````
# 6. MESSAGE DEPLOYMENT PLAN

## Day 1 — Warm contacts

Send today.

```text
Dear [Name], I am launching TenderProof OS, a focused bid-support service for donor-funded consulting opportunities. The first offer is a 48–72 hour Rapid Bid Diagnostic covering ToR analysis, bid/no-bid, compliance matrix, CV matching, methodology outline, and submission risk flags. Are you or your team reviewing any active tender where this could help?
```

## Day 2 — Cold LinkedIn contacts

Send after connection acceptance.

```text
Thanks for connecting, [Name]. I support donor-funded proposal teams with rapid ToR diagnostics, compliance mapping, CV matching, methodology outlines, and bid risk review. Are you currently reviewing any ADB/WB/EU/GIZ/USAID/UN opportunity where a 48–72 hour diagnostic would help?
```

## Day 2–3 — Bangladesh firms

```text
Dear [Name], I support Bangladesh consulting firms preparing donor-funded bids. My 48–72 hour diagnostic covers ToR analysis, bid/no-bid, compliance matrix, CV matching, methodology outline, and risk flags. Is your firm reviewing any active tender?
```

## Day 3–4 — International firms

```text
Dear [Name], I support international firms bidding in Bangladesh/South Asia with ToR analysis, local context, compliance matrix, CV/expert matching, methodology outline, and bid risk review. Are you reviewing any Bangladesh opportunity now?
```

## Day 4 — Team leaders

```text
Dear [Name], I help senior consultants and team leaders align CVs and technical positioning with donor ToRs. Are you currently being proposed for any ADB/WB/EU/GIZ/USAID assignment?
```

## Day 4–5 — Proposal managers

```text
Hi [Name], I support proposal managers under deadline pressure with ToR extraction, compliance matrices, CV matching, methodology outlines, and proposal QA. Are you handling any active proposal where extra diagnostic support would reduce risk?
```

## First follow-up — Day 3 after first message

```text
Dear [Name], just following up. The diagnostic is useful when a team has a live ToR but needs fast clarity on bid fit, compliance gaps, CV alignment, proposal structure, and risks. Are you currently reviewing any donor-funded opportunity where this could help?
```

## Second follow-up — Day 6 after first message

```text
Dear [Name], one common issue is that teams start writing before mapping the ToR, evaluation criteria, expert requirements, and compliance risks. Should I send you the one-page TenderProof diagnostic note for future use?
```

## Final follow-up — Day 10 after first message

```text
Dear [Name], I will close the loop for now. If your team later reviews an ADB, World Bank, EU, GIZ, FCDO, USAID, UN, or similar donor-funded opportunity, feel free to send the ToR and deadline. I can confirm quickly whether a 48–72 hour diagnostic would help.
```

# 7. REPLY-HANDLING PLAYBOOK

## “Interesting, send details.”

```text
Thanks, [Name]. I am sharing the one-page note. The diagnostic is a 48–72 hour review covering ToR analysis, bid/no-bid recommendation, compliance matrix, CV/expert matching, methodology outline, and risk flags.

If you have an active opportunity, please send the ToR and deadline. I can confirm the right package and price after checking the scope.
```

## “How much?”

```text
The 48-hour diagnostic usually ranges from BDT 50,000–100,000 for Bangladesh clients and USD 750–1,500 for international clients.

The 72-hour deeper diagnostic usually ranges from BDT 100,000–150,000+ or USD 1,500–2,500 depending on ToR complexity, number of CVs, and deadline.

If you send the ToR and deadline, I can confirm the exact quote.
```

## “Can you share a sample?”

```text
I can share the structure of the diagnostic output, but I do not provide unpaid ToR reviews because the analysis itself is the core work.

The diagnostic normally includes: opportunity summary, bid/no-bid score, compliance matrix, CV match table, methodology outline, risk flags, and next-step recommendation.
```

## “We do not have a tender now.”

```text
No problem. The best time to use this is when a live ToR arrives or when your team is shortlisted and needs proposal architecture quickly.

Should I send the one-page note so you can keep it for the next donor opportunity?
```

## “We have a tender but deadline is very close.”

```text
Please send the ToR, deadline, available CVs, and any forms immediately. I will check whether a 48-hour diagnostic is feasible.

If the deadline is too close, I may recommend a reduced-scope version focused only on bid/no-bid, compliance risks, CV fit, and immediate proposal structure.
```

## “Can you do the whole proposal?”

```text
Yes, if the diagnostic shows the opportunity is worth pursuing and the timeline is feasible. My usual sequence is: first diagnostic, then full technical proposal support.

Please send the ToR, deadline, CVs, past references, and existing draft if any. I will confirm whether we should begin with a diagnostic or move directly into a proposal sprint.
```

## “Can you join as an expert?”

```text
Possibly, depending on the role, timeline, conflict issues, and fit with my expertise. Please send the ToR and the proposed expert role. I can review whether I fit as an expert or whether TenderProof support is more appropriate.
```

## “Can we pay after submission?”

```text
For first-time clients, the diagnostic is paid upfront because it requires immediate time-blocking and rapid delivery. For full proposal support after the diagnostic, milestone payments can be considered.
```

## “This is expensive.”

```text
I understand. The diagnostic is priced as a decision and risk-reduction tool. It can save several days of senior staff time and identify compliance or CV risks before a team invests in full proposal writing.

If budget is tight, we can reduce scope to the 48-hour version.
```

## “Let us discuss later.”

```text
Sure. When would be the right time to reconnect? Also, if a live ToR appears before then, you can send the ToR and deadline directly, and I can quickly confirm whether a diagnostic would help.
```

## No reply after seen

Day 3:

```text
Dear [Name], just checking if your team has any active donor tender where a rapid ToR/compliance/CV diagnostic would be useful.
```

Day 7:

```text
Dear [Name], should I send the one-page TenderProof diagnostic note for future reference?
```

Day 10:

```text
Dear [Name], I will close the loop for now. Feel free to send a ToR later if your team needs rapid bid/no-bid or proposal-readiness support.
```

Tender/Opportunity:
Donor:
Deadline:
Submission Type:
Bidding Role:
Decision Maker Present? Yes/No
Budget Approved? Yes/No/Unknown

## Pre-call checklist

```text
[ ] Reviewed lead profile
[ ] Checked organization website/LinkedIn
[ ] Checked donor/sector fit
[ ] Prepared 20-minute call agenda
[ ] Ready to ask for ToR and deadline
[ ] Ready to quote 48h/72h range
```

## Questions to ask

```text
1. Which donor/client issued the ToR?
2. What is the deadline?
3. What stage is this: EOI, RFP, technical, financial, expert role?
4. Are you bidding as lead, partner, sub, or expert?
5. What is the main concern?
6. Do you have CVs ready?
7. Do you have past references ready?
8. Do you have donor forms/templates?
9. Has a compliance matrix been prepared?
10. Has the methodology been drafted?
11. Who is making the decision?
12. Is budget approved?
13. What would be most useful in 48–72 hours?
```

## Scoring during call

| Item | Score 1–5 |
|---|---:|
| Urgency |  |
| Buyer authority |  |
| Ability to pay |  |
| ToR/document readiness |  |
| Sector fit |  |
| Repeat potential |  |

Total:

```excel
=SUM(scores)
```

## Urgent pain indicators

```text
[ ] Deadline within 2 weeks
[ ] No compliance matrix
[ ] CVs not aligned
[ ] Methodology not drafted
[ ] Expert gap exists
[ ] Shortlisted already
[ ] International partner needs local input
[ ] Proposal team overloaded
[ ] Past similar bid lost
[ ] Decision-maker involved
```

## Close probability score

| Score | Meaning |
|---:|---|
| 24–30 | High probability; ask for documents and quote |
| 18–23 | Medium; send offer note and follow up |
| 12–17 | Low; nurture |
| Below 12 | Park |

## Recommended offer

| Situation | Offer |
|---|---|
| Deadline very close | 48-hour diagnostic |
| Serious bid with enough documents | 72-hour diagnostic |
| They ask for whole proposal | Diagnostic first, then proposal sprint |
| Individual expert role | CV-ToR diagnostic |
| No active tender | Keep on list and ask for referral |

## Next action

```text
[ ] Send document request
[ ] Send one-page offer
[ ] Send quote
[ ] Send payment terms
[ ] Schedule follow-up
[ ] Park lead
```

## Follow-up message after call

```text
Dear [Name], thank you for the call.

Based on our discussion, the next step is to review the ToR and available documents and confirm the diagnostic scope.

Please send:
- ToR/RFP/EOI
- submission deadline
- available CVs
- past project references
- donor forms/templates
- existing draft, if any
- intended bidding role

After reviewing the documents, I will confirm whether the 48-hour or 72-hour diagnostic is appropriate and share the exact quote and payment terms.

Best,
Syful
```

# 9. CLIENT CLOSE FLOW

| Step | Action | Message/Script | Decision Rule | CRM Log |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Reply received | Respond within same day | “Thanks, [Name]. Is there an active ToR/deadline, or should I send the one-page note for future use?” | If active tender → qualify. If no tender → nurture. | Status = Replied |
| 2. Qualify | Ask about donor, deadline, documents, role, budget | “Which donor/client, what deadline, and are CVs/forms ready?” | If deadline + ToR exist → call/document request. | Current Tender Signal, Urgency Score |
| 3. Call | Run 20-minute call | Use call script | If score ≥18 → ask for documents. If below → nurture. | Call Booked/Completed |
| 4. Document request | Ask for ToR, CVs, references | Use document request message | If documents sent → quote. If not → follow up in 24h. | Documents Requested/Received |
| 5. Quote | Send package and fee | “Based on scope, I recommend [48h/72h]. Fee is [amount].” | If accepts → payment. If objects → reduce scope, not price first. | Quote Sent, Price Quoted |
| 6. Payment | Send payment terms | Use payment terms reply | Do not start before payment for first-time clients. | Payment Status |
| 7. Intake | Confirm complete documents | “Timeline starts after payment and complete documents.” | If incomplete → pause. | Client Intake updated |
| 8. Delivery starts | Create project folder and begin | “Work has started. Delivery expected by [date/time].” | Start only after payment + documents. | Delivery Status |

## Sample compliance matrix

| Requirement | Status | Risk | Action needed |
|---|---|---|---|

## Sample risk flags

| Risk | Severity | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| Education Data Specialist weak | High | Replace or add co-expert |
| Page limit risk | High | Create section-level page budget |
| Generic methodology risk | Medium | Use data-system diagnostic framework |
| Training plan underdeveloped | Medium | Add capacity-building module |
| CV language too broad | Medium | Rewrite CVs against ToR keywords |

## Sample methodology outline

```text
1. Understanding of the assignment
The assignment requires strengthening secondary education data systems through diagnostic review, stakeholder consultation, institutional assessment, roadmap development, and capacity building.

2. Technical approach
The proposed approach combines education system analysis, data governance review, institutional process mapping, M&E framework assessment, and costed roadmap development.

3. Workstream 1: Inception and diagnostic framework
Confirm scope, stakeholders, data sources, deliverable expectations, and assessment tools.

4. Workstream 2: Education data system diagnostic
Review existing data flows, reporting systems, institutional responsibilities, data quality, and bottlenecks.

5. Workstream 3: Roadmap and capacity-building plan
Develop phased recommendations, training modules, governance actions, and implementation roadmap.

6. Quality assurance
Use document review, stakeholder validation, internal peer review, and deliverable quality checklist.
```

## Final recommendation

**Pursue with fixes.**  
The opportunity fits the firm’s education, M&E, and economics experience. However, the Education Data Specialist gap and page-limit risk must be addressed before full proposal drafting. The next step should be a 7–10 day Technical Proposal Sprint focused on methodology, expert replacement or CV strengthening, compliance completion, and final QA.

# 13. VALIDATION DECISION TREE

## 0 replies after 50 messages

Action:

1. Stop cold expansion for 24 hours.  
2. Switch to warm contacts and referrals.  
3. Shorten message.  
4. Ask only: “Are you reviewing any active donor tender?”  
5. Improve targeting toward proposal managers and active bidders.

Do not change the offer yet.

---

## Replies but no calls

Action:

1. Ask for a 15-minute fit call, not 30 minutes.  
2. Offer two time slots.  
3. Ask whether they have an active ToR.  
4. Send one-page PDF only after they show interest.

Script:

```text
Would a 15-minute fit call help? I can quickly confirm whether the diagnostic is relevant for your current tender.
```

---

## Calls but no payment

Action:

1. Ask if budget is approved.  
2. Send fixed package and payment terms.  
3. Reduce scope to 48-hour version if price is issue.  
4. Do not start unpaid review.

Decision:

- If no budget: park.  
- If budget but hesitation: send quote deadline.  
- If wants free sample: refuse politely.

---

## Payment but weak delivery

Action:

1. Use QC checklist before delivery.  
2. Keep scope narrow.  
3. Do not overpromise.  
4. Offer correction call if factual issues exist.

After delivery:

- Capture gaps.  
- Improve template.  
- Do not scale outreach until delivery quality is stable.

---

## Delivery but no upsell

Action:

1. Strengthen next-step recommendation.  
2. Identify 3 concrete gaps.  
3. Offer fixed Technical Proposal Sprint.  
4. Ask directly: “Do you want me to support the next stage?”

---

## Upsell but no retainer

Action:

1. Do not push retainer too early.  
2. Ask if they bid regularly.  
3. Offer monthly tender watch only after 2 interactions.  
4. Build trust through delivery first.

---

## Strong demand from one segment

Action:

1. Prioritize that segment for next 14 days.  
2. Rewrite message for that segment.  
3. Build one sector-specific sample.  
4. Pause weaker segments.

---

## Demand from international firms

Action:

1. Quote international pricing.  
2. Emphasize Bangladesh/local context.  
3. Ask for longer pipeline.  
4. Offer diagnostic + local proposal support.

Do not charge local rates.

---

## Demand only from individual consultants

Action:

1. Offer smaller CV-ToR diagnostic only if paid.  
2. Use them as referral sources to firms.  
3. Do not let individual CV work consume all time.  
4. Prioritize team leaders with active roles.

---

# 15. NON-NEGOTIABLE RULEBOOK

## Daily must-do

- Add at least 10 leads.  
- Send at least 10 messages.  
- Follow up all due leads.  
- Log every contact in CRM.  
- Ask every interested person about active ToR and deadline.  
- Protect at least 30 minutes for asset improvement.  
- Capture every objection.  

## Must not do

- Do not build SaaS.  
- Do not build dashboard app.  
- Do not redesign brand repeatedly.  
- Do not create new product ideas.  
- Do not accept unpaid ToR reviews.  
- Do not chase tenders without a paying client.  
- Do not write full proposal before payment.  
- Do not guarantee tender success.  
- Do not fabricate credentials, experience, or eligibility.  

## Reject clients when

- They want free diagnostic.  
- They want success-fee only.  
- They ask for fabricated experience.  
- They have no ToR, no deadline, no decision-maker.  
- They cannot pay upfront for diagnostic.  
- They create urgent chaos without documents.  
- They negotiate before understanding scope.  

## Raise price when

- International firm is buying.  
- Deadline is under 48 hours.  
- More than 5 CVs need review.  
- ToR is complex or multi-country.  
- Proposal has high contract value.  
- Client requests weekend/late-night rush work.  
- Diagnostic includes methodology deeper than agreed.  

## Create software only when

- Same workflow has repeated 5–10 times.  
- Clients have paid for the manual version.  
- You know which fields repeat.  
- Delivery bottleneck is real.  
- Automation will reduce time, not distract from selling.  

## Ignore distractions when

- It does not help get a paid diagnostic.  
- It does not help deliver a current diagnostic.  
- It does not improve a reusable asset.  
- It does not capture proposal intelligence.  
- It creates branding, design, or platform work before revenue.  

## Park other ideas when

- They are not TenderProof OS.  
- They do not lead to paid bid diagnostics.  
- They require new market education.  
- They require product development before cash.  
- They are exciting but not urgent.  

Your next execution move:

1. Open LinkedIn.  
2. Update profile.  
3. Open Google Sheets.  
4. Build the cockpit.  
5. Send 10 warm messages.  
6. Log everything.  
7. Follow up on Day 3.
````

````
### Corrected formulas

Weighted Score, in `E2`:

```excel
=C2*D2
```

Total weighted score for one tender, assuming rows 2–11:

```excel
=SUM(E2:E11)
```

Decision rule:

```excel
=IF(SUM(E2:E11)>=4,"Pursue",IF(SUM(E2:E11)>=3.25,"Pursue with fixes",IF(SUM(E2:E11)>=2.5,"Only with partner or reduced role","Reject")))
```

---

# 2. GOOGLE FORM FINAL BUILD

## Form title

**TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic — Client Intake Form**

## Form description

Please complete this form to request a 48–72 hour diagnostic for a donor-funded tender or proposal opportunity. The diagnostic may include ToR analysis, bid/no-bid recommendation, compliance matrix, CV/expert matching, methodology outline, and proposal risk flags. Full proposal writing is separate and quoted after the diagnostic or after scope review.

Work begins only after scope confirmation, payment, and receipt of required documents.

---

## Section 1: Client information

| Question | Type | Required | Options / help text | Logic |
|---|---|---:|---|---|
| Name | Short answer | Yes | Your full name | None |
| Organization | Short answer | Yes | Firm, NGO, think tank, or individual consultant name | None |
| Role/designation | Short answer | Yes | Example: Managing Director, Proposal Manager, Team Leader | None |
| Email | Short answer | Yes | Use the email for follow-up and documents | None |
| WhatsApp/phone | Short answer | Yes | Include country code if outside Bangladesh | None |

---

## Section 2: Tender information

| Question | Type | Required | Options / help text | Logic |
|---|---|---:|---|---|
| Tender title | Short answer | Yes | Copy the title from the ToR/RFP | None |
| Donor/client | Short answer | Yes | Example: ADB, World Bank, EU, GIZ, USAID, UN | None |
| Country/region | Short answer | Yes | Country or region of assignment | None |
| Sector | Dropdown | Yes | Education, Governance, Climate, Infrastructure, Private Sector Development, Evaluation/M&E, Public Finance, CBA/Economics, Other | None |
| Submission deadline | Date | Yes | Final submission deadline | None |
| Submission type | Multiple choice | Yes | EOI, REOI, RFP, Technical Proposal, Financial Proposal, Individual Consultant, Other | None |

| What is your main concern about this opportunity? | Paragraph | Yes | Example: compliance, CV fit, methodology, deadline, expert gaps, bid/no-bid decision | None |
| Who will decide whether to proceed with this diagnostic? | Short answer | Yes | Name or role of decision-maker | None |

|---|---|---:|---|---|

| Upload ToR/RFP/EOI document | File upload | Yes | Upload PDF or Word document | Show only if previous answer is Yes |
| Do you have CVs ready? | Multiple choice | Yes | Yes, No, Partially | If Yes/Partially → show CV upload |
| Upload available CVs | File upload | No | Upload CVs if available | Conditional |

# 3. ONE-PAGE OFFER PDF FINAL

## **TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic**  
### **48–72 Hour ToR, Compliance, CV Match & Bid Strategy Review for Donor-Funded Proposals**

Donor-funded tenders are often lost before the full proposal is written. The ToR is complex, compliance requirements are scattered, CVs may not fully match the expert criteria, and teams often commit time before making a disciplined bid decision.

**TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic** gives your team a structured, fast, and commercially practical review before full proposal preparation begins.

## What you receive

Within 48–72 hours, you receive a diagnostic package covering:

- ToR opportunity summary  
- Bid/no-bid recommendation  
- Compliance matrix  
- Expert and CV matching review  
- CV gap analysis  
- Proposal skeleton  
- Methodology outline  
- Key risk flags  
- Clarification question suggestions  
- 45–60 minute bid strategy call  

## Delivery options

| Option | Best for | Timeline |
|---|---|---|
| **48-Hour Rapid Diagnostic** | Urgent bid/no-bid and risk snapshot | 48 hours |
| **72-Hour Deep Diagnostic** | Serious bidders preparing for technical proposal support | 72 hours |

## Pricing

| Client type | Price range |
|---|---:|
| Bangladesh/local clients | BDT 50,000–150,000+ |
| International clients | USD 750–2,500 |
| Urgent delivery | Rush premium may apply |

Final price depends on ToR complexity, number of CVs, deadline, and required depth.

## Required documents

Please send:

- ToR / RFP / EOI / REOI document  
- Submission deadline  
- Available CVs  
- Past project references  
- Donor/client forms or templates  
- Evaluation criteria, if separate  
- Consortium or partner information  
- Intended bidding role  
- Existing draft, if available  

## Boundary

This diagnostic does **not** include full technical proposal writing, financial proposal preparation, donor portal submission, or guaranteed tender success. Full proposal support can be quoted separately after the diagnostic.

## Why Syful

Mohammad Syful Hoque combines donor-funded consulting, economic analysis, CBA, policy research, education, climate, governance, private-sector development, evaluation, and Bangladesh / Global South experience. TenderProof OS helps bidders turn complex ToRs into structured, compliant, and competitive proposal systems.

## Call to action

Send the ToR and deadline to request a diagnostic quote.

**Mohammad Syful Hoque**  
Founder, TenderProof OS / ProposalGuard

# 6. SALES CALL KIT

## Printable 1-page sales call sheet

### Opening script

Thanks for taking the time, [Name]. I will keep this practical. I want to understand the tender, deadline, available documents, and whether a 48–72 hour TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic would genuinely help. If it is not a fit, I will say so directly.

---

## 12 questions

1. Which donor/client issued the ToR?  
2. What is the submission deadline?  
3. What stage is this: EOI, RFP, technical, financial, or expert role?  
4. Are you bidding as lead, partner, sub-consultant, consortium member, or individual expert?  
5. What is the main concern right now?  
6. Do you already meet the eligibility requirements?  
7. Do you have CVs ready?  
8. Do you have relevant past project references?  
9. Has a compliance matrix been prepared?  
10. Has the methodology section been drafted?  
11. Who will decide whether to proceed?  
12. Is budget already approved for bid support?

---

## Urgency checklist

Mark yes/no:

- Deadline within 2 weeks  
- No compliance matrix  
- CVs not aligned  
- Methodology not drafted  
- Expert gap exists  
- Shortlisted already  
- International partner needs local input  
- Proposal team overloaded  
- Similar bid lost before  
- Decision-maker involved

| Sector fit |  |

| **Total** |  |

| Total score | Probability | Action |
|---:|---|---|
| 24–30 | High | Ask for documents and quote |
| 18–23 | Medium | Send offer note and follow up |
| 12–17 | Low | Nurture |
| Below 12 | Park |

## Recommended offer rule

| Situation | Offer |
|---|---|
| Deadline very close | 48-hour diagnostic |
| Serious bid with enough documents | 72-hour diagnostic |
| They ask for whole proposal | Diagnostic first, then proposal sprint |
| Individual expert role | CV-ToR diagnostic |
| No active tender | Keep on list and ask for referral |

## Invoice description

**TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic**  
48–72 hour ToR, compliance, CV/expert match, methodology outline, bid/no-bid, and proposal risk diagnostic for [Tender Title].

**Client:** [Client/Firm]  
**Package:** [48-hour / 72-hour]  
**Fee:** [Amount]  
**Timeline:** [48/72 hours] from payment confirmation and receipt of complete documents.

---

## Engagement terms

1. Work starts after payment confirmation and receipt of required documents.  
2. Diagnostic fees are paid 100% upfront for first-time clients.  
3. Delivery timeline begins only after all required documents are received.  
4. The diagnostic includes only the deliverables agreed under the selected package.  
5. Full technical proposal writing is not included unless separately contracted.  
6. Client is responsible for final submission to the donor/client.  
7. TenderProof OS does not guarantee tender success.  
8. Additional work beyond agreed scope will be quoted separately.  
9. Urgent delivery may include a rush premium.  
10. Client is responsible for accuracy of submitted CVs, references, credentials, and organizational information.

---

## Scope boundary

The diagnostic includes:

- ToR review  
- bid/no-bid assessment  
- compliance matrix  
- CV/expert match review  
- methodology outline  
- proposal skeleton  
- risk flags  
- one diagnostic call  

The diagnostic does not include:

- full technical proposal drafting  
- financial proposal preparation  
- full CV rewriting  
- graphic design/formatting  
- donor portal submission  
- partner negotiation  
- guaranteed tender success  
- fabricated credentials or experience  

---

## No-refund clause

Because the diagnostic is time-sensitive knowledge work, no refund applies after work has started. Work is considered started once the ToR, CVs, or supporting documents have been reviewed.

Refund may be considered only if the client cancels before document review begins.

---

## Confidentiality note

All client documents, ToRs, CVs, proposal drafts, project references, and internal discussions will be treated as confidential. TenderProof OS will not share client documents externally without written permission.

Anonymized learning may be captured internally to improve templates, compliance checklists, and proposal quality systems, without disclosing client identity or confidential project information.

---

## Ethical boundary

TenderProof OS does not fabricate credentials, project experience, CV claims, eligibility information, donor references, contract values, or institutional experience. All proposal content must be based on accurate and verifiable information provided by the client.

---

## Full proposal upsell paragraph

After the diagnostic, TenderProof OS can support the next stage through a **Technical Proposal Sprint**. This includes full technical proposal structure, methodology drafting, workplan logic, CV alignment, past experience matrix, compliance completion, and final proposal QA. The scope and fee will be quoted separately based on deadline, proposal complexity, number of experts, and required deliverables.

---

# 11. QUALITY CONTROL AND ERROR CHECK

| Issue checked | Correction |
|---|---|
| Inconsistent pricing | Final range fixed: Bangladesh BDT 50,000–150,000+; international USD 750–2,500 |
| CTA unclear | Final CTA: “Send the ToR and deadline to request a diagnostic quote” |
| Weak wording | Removed “maybe,” “try,” “can possibly help” from client-facing offer |
| Duplicated messages | Final message pack selects one best version per situation |
| Formula errors | Dashboard formulas now use exact date columns |
| Mixed currency revenue | Dashboard separates BDT and USD revenue |
| Missing payment boundary | Added 100% upfront for first-time diagnostics |
| Missing refund boundary | Added no refund after work starts |
| Missing ethical boundary | Added no fabricated credentials or experience |
| Missing full proposal boundary | Final offer states full proposal writing is separate |
| Missing follow-up steps | Added first, second, final follow-up sequence |
| Buyer confusion risk | Final materials say diagnostic first, full proposal separate |
| Scope creep risk | Scope boundary added in close kit |
| Confidentiality concern | Confidentiality note added |
| Dashboard ambiguity | Manual fields marked where formulas are not reliable |
| Call conversion weakness | Sales call sheet includes scoring and close rule |

Corrected final version is the pack above.
````

````
# 0. ERROR CHECK BEFORE BUILD

| Check | Result / correction |
|---|---|
| Invalid Google Sheet formulas | Corrected. Formulas now reference final column positions exactly. |
| Lead Score formula | Uses columns Q, R, S = Offer Fit Score + Urgency Score + Ability to Pay Score. Formula goes in U. |
| Days Remaining formula | Uses Deadline in column H and Days Remaining in column I. |
| Weighted Score formula | Uses Weight in column C and Score in column D. Formula goes in E. |
| Weekly Dashboard formulas | Corrected to use final CRM date/status columns. |
| BDT/USD revenue | Separated into two dashboard columns. |
| Google Form conditional logic | Apps Script can create sections/questions, but advanced show/hide logic is limited. Manual branching notes are included. |
| File upload questions | Apps Script may fail on some accounts. Script includes fallback paragraph fields for Drive links. |
| Form responses to Client Intake | Script connects Form to the Cockpit spreadsheet and also installs a trigger to copy key responses into the `Client Intake` tab. |
| Naming conflicts | Sheet creation always creates a new spreadsheet. Drive folders use get-or-create logic. Docs may create new copies if the function is run again. |
| Missing payment boundary | Included in engagement terms and quote template. |
| Missing ethical boundary | Included: no fabricated credentials, CV claims, experience, references, or eligibility. |

---

# 1–4. GOOGLE WORKSPACE BUILD KIT — GOOGLE APPS SCRIPT

Paste the full code below into Apps Script as `Code.gs`.

```javascript id="tenderproof-build-kit"
const TP = {
  cockpitName: "TenderProof OS Execution Cockpit",
  rootFolderName: "TenderProof_OS",
  sampleClientFolder: "ClientName_TenderShortName"
};

/**
 * RUN THIS FIRST if you want everything created.
 */
function runAllTenderProofSetup() {
  const ss = buildCockpit();
  const root = buildDriveFolders();
  buildDocsTemplates();
  buildIntakeForm();

  Logger.log("TenderProof setup complete.");
  Logger.log("Cockpit URL: " + ss.getUrl());
  Logger.log("Drive root folder URL: " + root.getUrl());
}

/**
 * 1. GOOGLE SHEETS — COCKPIT BUILDER
 */
function buildCockpit() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.create(TP.cockpitName);
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("COCKPIT_ID", ss.getId());

  const definitions = getSheetDefinitions_();
  const defaultSheet = ss.getSheets()[0];

  definitions.forEach((def, i) => {
    const sh = i === 0 ? defaultSheet.setName(def.name) : ss.insertSheet(def.name);
    sh.clear();

    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, def.headers.length).setValues([def.headers]);

    if (def.sampleRows.length > 0) {
      sh.getRange(2, 1, def.sampleRows.length, def.headers.length).setValues(def.sampleRows);
    }

    formatSheet_(sh, def.headers.length);
    applyValidations_(sh, def.headers, def.validations || {});
  });

  applyCockpitFormulas_(ss);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return ss;
}

  headers = [
    "Quote ID","Date","Client Name","Organization","Client Type","Country","Package","Price Quoted",
    "Currency","Discount Offered","Final Price","Payment Terms","Invoice Sent","Invoice Date",
    "Payment Received","Payment Date","Objection","Outcome","Follow-Up Date","Notes"
  ];

  sheets.push({
    name: "Pricing Tracker",
    headers,
    sampleRows: [
      row_(headers, {
        "Quote ID":"Q001","Date":today,"Client Name":"Sample Client 1","Organization":"Sample Consulting Ltd",
        "Client Type":"Bangladesh Firm","Country":"Bangladesh","Package":"72h Diagnostic","Price Quoted":150000,
        "Currency":"BDT","Discount Offered":"No","Final Price":150000,"Payment Terms":"100 percent upfront",
        "Invoice Sent":"No","Payment Received":"No","Objection":"None","Outcome":"Pending",
        "Follow-Up Date":addDays_(today, 1),"Notes":"Awaiting confirmation"
      }),
      row_(headers, {
        "Quote ID":"Q002","Date":today,"Client Name":"Sample Client 2","Organization":"International Firm Ltd",
        "Client Type":"International Firm","Country":"UK","Package":"48h Diagnostic","Price Quoted":1500,
        "Currency":"USD","Discount Offered":"No","Final Price":1500,"Payment Terms":"100 percent upfront",
        "Invoice Sent":"No","Payment Received":"No","Objection":"None","Outcome":"Pending",
        "Follow-Up Date":addDays_(today, 1),"Notes":"Awaiting ToR"
      }),
      row_(headers, {
        "Quote ID":"Q003","Date":today,"Client Name":"Sample Client 3","Organization":"Independent Consultant",
        "Client Type":"Senior Consultant","Country":"Bangladesh","Package":"CV ToR Diagnostic","Price Quoted":50000,
        "Currency":"BDT","Discount Offered":"No","Final Price":50000,"Payment Terms":"100 percent upfront",
        "Invoice Sent":"No","Payment Received":"No","Objection":"Price high","Outcome":"Negotiating",
        "Follow-Up Date":addDays_(today, 1),"Notes":"May reduce scope"
      })
    ],
    validations: {
      "Package":["48h Diagnostic","72h Diagnostic","Full Proposal Sprint","Monthly Bid Desk","CV ToR Diagnostic"],
      "Currency":["BDT","USD","EUR"],
      "Discount Offered":["Yes","No"],
      "Invoice Sent":["Yes","No"],
      "Payment Received":["Yes","No"],
      "Outcome":["Pending","Accepted","Rejected","Negotiating","Paid","Lost"]

  });

  // 10. IP Capture Database
  headers = [
    "IP ID","Date Captured","Project/Tender ID","Donor","Sector","Country","Pattern Type","Pattern Description",
    "Expert Requirement","Evaluation Criteria","Methodology Module","Compliance Risk","CV Language Pattern",
    "QA Failure","Pricing Signal","Win/Loss Lesson","Reusable Asset Created","Notes"
  ];

  sheets.push({
    name: "IP Capture Database",
    headers,
    sampleRows: [
      row_(headers, {
        "IP ID":"IP001","Date Captured":today,"Project/Tender ID":"T001","Donor":"ADB","Sector":"Education",
        "Country":"Bangladesh","Pattern Type":"Expert Pattern",
        "Pattern Description":"Team Leader requires 15 years and donor education reform experience",
        "Expert Requirement":"Team Leader 15 years","Evaluation Criteria":"High technical score",
        "Methodology Module":"Education reform methodology","Compliance Risk":"CV donor language weak",
        "CV Language Pattern":"Led donor funded education reform","QA Failure":"None",
        "Pricing Signal":"BDT 150k quote","Win/Loss Lesson":"Pending","Reusable Asset Created":"Yes",
        "Notes":"Add to CV matcher"
      }),
      row_(headers, {
        "IP ID":"IP002","Date Captured":today,"Project/Tender ID":"T001","Donor":"ADB","Sector":"Education",
        "Country":"Bangladesh","Pattern Type":"Compliance Risk",
        "Pattern Description":"Strict 30 page technical proposal limit","Expert Requirement":"None",
        "Evaluation Criteria":"Page control","Methodology Module":"Page budget module",
        "Compliance Risk":"Page limit risk","CV Language Pattern":"None","QA Failure":"Potential over length",
        "Pricing Signal":"None","Win/Loss Lesson":"Pending","Reusable Asset Created":"Yes",
        "Notes":"Add page budget template"
      }),
      row_(headers, {
        "IP ID":"IP003","Date Captured":today,"Project/Tender ID":"T001","Donor":"ADB","Sector":"Education",
        "Country":"Bangladesh","Pattern Type":"Methodology Module",
        "Pattern Description":"Needs inception diagnostic validation finalization sequence",
        "Expert Requirement":"None","Evaluation Criteria":"Methodology quality",
        "Methodology Module":"Education TA workstream module","Compliance Risk":"None",
        "CV Language Pattern":"None","QA Failure":"None","Pricing Signal":"None","Win/Loss Lesson":"Pending",
        "Reusable Asset Created":"Yes","Notes":"Add methodology bank entry"
      })
    ],
    validations: {
      "Pattern Type":["Donor Pattern","Expert Pattern","Evaluation Criteria","Methodology Module","Compliance Risk","CV Language","QA Failure","Pricing Signal","Win/Loss Lesson"],
      "Reusable Asset Created":["Yes","No"]
    }
  });

  // 11. Weekly Dashboard
  headers = [
    "Week Start Date","Week End Date","New Leads Added","Messages Sent","Follow-Ups Sent","Replies Received",
    "Calls Booked","Calls Completed","Quotes Sent","Paid Diagnostics Sold","Revenue Collected BDT",
    "Revenue Collected USD","Diagnostics Delivered","Full Proposal Upsells Offered","Retainer Conversations",
    "Templates Improved","Methodology Modules Added","IP Entries Captured","Top Objection","Main Learning","Next Week Priority"
  ];

  sheets.push({
    name: "Weekly Dashboard",
    headers,
    sampleRows: [
      row_(headers, {
        "Week Start Date":today,"Week End Date":addDays_(today, 6),
        "Diagnostics Delivered":0,"Full Proposal Upsells Offered":0,"Retainer Conversations":0,
        "Templates Improved":0,"Methodology Modules Added":0,"Top Objection":"Price high",
        "Main Learning":"Test warm contacts","Next Week Priority":"Focus warm leads"
      }),
      row_(headers, {
        "Week Start Date":addDays_(today, 7),"Week End Date":addDays_(today, 13)
      }),
      row_(headers, {
        "Week Start Date":addDays_(today, 14),"Week End Date":addDays_(today, 20)
      })
    ],
    validations: {}
  });

  return sheets;
}

function applyCockpitFormulas_(ss) {
  const leadSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Leads CRM");
  leadSheet.getRange("U2:U501").setFormulaR1C1('=IF(COUNTA(RC[-4]:RC[-2])=0,"",SUM(RC[-4]:RC[-2]))');

  const tenderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Tender Tracker");
  tenderSheet.getRange("I2:I501").setFormulaR1C1('=IF(RC[-1]="","",RC[-1]-TODAY())');

  const scoreSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Bid/No-Bid Scorecard");
  scoreSheet.getRange("E2:E501").setFormulaR1C1('=IF(OR(RC[-2]="",RC[-1]=""),"",RC[-2]*RC[-1])');

  const dash = ss.getSheetByName("Weekly Dashboard");

  for (let r = 2; r <= 20; r++) {
    dash.getRange(r, 3).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!B:B,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!B:B,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 4).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!W:W,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!W:W,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 5).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!Z:Z,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!Z:Z,"<="&B${r})+COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AA:AA,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AA:AA,"<="&B${r})+COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AB:AB,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AB:AB,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 6).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AC:AC,"Yes",'Leads CRM'!AD:AD,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AD:AD,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 7).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AE:AE,"Yes",'Leads CRM'!AF:AF,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AF:AF,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 8).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AG:AG,"Yes",'Leads CRM'!AH:AH,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AH:AH,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 9).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AI:AI,"Yes",'Leads CRM'!AJ:AJ,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AJ:AJ,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 10).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Pricing Tracker'!G:G,"*Diagnostic*",'Pricing Tracker'!O:O,"Yes",'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,">="&A${r},'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 11).setFormula(`=SUMIFS('Pricing Tracker'!K:K,'Pricing Tracker'!I:I,"BDT",'Pricing Tracker'!O:O,"Yes",'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,">="&A${r},'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 12).setFormula(`=SUMIFS('Pricing Tracker'!K:K,'Pricing Tracker'!I:I,"USD",'Pricing Tracker'!O:O,"Yes",'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,">="&A${r},'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 18).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('IP Capture Database'!B:B,">="&A${r},'IP Capture Database'!B:B,"<="&B${r})`);
  }
}

function formatSheet_(sh, lastCol) {
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol)
    .setFontWeight("bold")
    .setBackground("#1f4e78")
    .setFontColor("#ffffff")
    .setWrap(true);

  sh.getDataRange().setWrap(true);
  sh.autoResizeColumns(1, lastCol);

  try {
    sh.getRange(1, 1, Math.max(1, sh.getLastRow()), lastCol).createFilter();
  } catch (e) {}

  sh.setRowHeight(1, 40);
}

function applyValidations_(sh, headers, validations) {
  Object.keys(validations).forEach(header => {
    const col = headers.indexOf(header) + 1;
    if (col <= 0) return;

    const rule = SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
      .requireValueInList(validations[header], true)
      .setAllowInvalid(false)
      .build();

    sh.getRange(2, col, 500, 1).setDataValidation(rule);
  });
}

function row_(headers, obj) {
  return headers.map(h => obj[h] !== undefined ? obj[h] : "");
}

function addDays_(date, days) {
  const d = new Date(date);
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + days);
  return d;
}

/**
 * 2. GOOGLE FORM BUILDER SCRIPT
 */
function buildIntakeForm() {
  const form = FormApp.create("TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic — Client Intake Form");
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("INTAKE_FORM_ID", form.getId());

  form.setDescription(
    "Please complete this form to request a 48–72 hour diagnostic for a donor-funded tender or proposal opportunity. " +
    "The diagnostic may include ToR analysis, bid/no-bid recommendation, compliance matrix, CV/expert matching, methodology outline, and proposal risk flags. " +
    "Full proposal writing is separate and quoted after the diagnostic or after scope review.\n\n" +
    "Work begins only after scope confirmation, payment, and receipt of required documents."
  );

  form.setConfirmationMessage(
    "Thank you. Your TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic request has been received. " +
    "Please also send the ToR and deadline directly to [Email] or WhatsApp [Phone] if not uploaded here. " +
    "I will review the scope and confirm the package, price, timeline, and payment terms before work begins."
  );

  // Section 1
  form.addSectionHeaderItem().setTitle("Section 1: Client information");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Name").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Your full name.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Organization").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Firm, NGO, think tank, or individual consultant name.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Role/designation").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Example: Managing Director, Proposal Manager, Team Leader.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Email").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Use the email for follow-up and documents.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("WhatsApp/phone").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Include country code if outside Bangladesh.");

  // Section 2
  form.addPageBreakItem().setTitle("Section 2: Tender information");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Tender title").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Copy the title from the ToR/RFP.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Donor/client").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Example: ADB, World Bank, EU, GIZ, USAID, UN.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Country/region").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Country or region of assignment.");
  form.addListItem()
    .setTitle("Sector")
    .setChoiceValues(["Education","Governance","Climate","Infrastructure","Private Sector Development","Evaluation/M&E","Public Finance","CBA/Economics","Other"])
    .setRequired(true);
  form.addDateItem().setTitle("Submission deadline").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Final submission deadline.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Submission type")
    .setChoiceValues(["EOI","REOI","RFP","Technical Proposal","Financial Proposal","Individual Consultant","Other"])
    .setRequired(true);

  // Section 3
  form.addPageBreakItem().setTitle("Section 3: Bidding role and concern");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Are you bidding as lead, partner, sub-consultant, consortium member, or individual expert?")
    .setChoiceValues(["Lead","Partner","Sub-consultant","Consortium Member","Individual Expert","Not Sure"])
    .setRequired(true);
  form.addParagraphTextItem()
    .setTitle("What is your main concern about this opportunity?")
    .setRequired(true)
    .setHelpText("Example: compliance, CV fit, methodology, deadline, expert gaps, bid/no-bid decision.");
  form.addTextItem()
    .setTitle("Who will decide whether to proceed with this diagnostic?")
    .setRequired(true)
    .setHelpText("Name or role of decision-maker.");

  // Section 4
  form.addPageBreakItem().setTitle("Section 4: Available documents");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Do you have the ToR/RFP/EOI document?")
    .setChoiceValues(["Yes","No"])
    .setRequired(true)
    .setHelpText("If no, complete the remaining questions and send the ToR when available.");

  addFileUploadOrFallback_(form, "Upload ToR/RFP/EOI document", true, "Upload PDF or Word document. If upload is unavailable, paste a Drive link.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you have CVs ready?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No","Partially"]).setRequired(true);
  addFileUploadOrFallback_(form, "Upload available CVs", false, "Upload CVs if available. If upload is unavailable, paste a Drive link.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you have past project references?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No","Partially"]).setRequired(true);
  addFileUploadOrFallback_(form, "Upload past project references", false, "Upload Word, Excel, or PDF if available. If upload is unavailable, paste a Drive link.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you have donor/client forms or templates?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No","Not Sure"]).setRequired(true);
  addFileUploadOrFallback_(form, "Upload donor/client forms/templates", false, "Upload technical proposal form, CV format, EOI form, etc. If upload is unavailable, paste a Drive link.");

  // Section 5
  form.addPageBreakItem().setTitle("Section 5: Existing work and package");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you already have a draft proposal?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No"]).setRequired(true);
  addFileUploadOrFallback_(form, "Upload existing draft", false, "Upload draft if available. If upload is unavailable, paste a Drive link.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Which diagnostic do you need?")
    .setChoiceValues(["48-hour Rapid Diagnostic","72-hour Deep Diagnostic","Not Sure"])
    .setRequired(true);
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Is budget approved for this diagnostic?")
    .setChoiceValues(["Yes","No","Need Approval"])
    .setRequired(true);
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Preferred payment method").setRequired(false).setHelpText("Bank transfer, mobile banking, international transfer, etc.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you require a confidentiality note/NDA?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No"]).setRequired(true);
  form.addParagraphTextItem().setTitle("Any other instruction or concern?").setRequired(false);

  // Section 6
  form.addPageBreakItem().setTitle("Section 6: No ToR available yet");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("When do you expect the ToR/RFP to be available?").setRequired(false).setHelpText("Provide expected date if known.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Would you like to receive the one-page TenderProof offer note for future use?")
    .setChoiceValues(["Yes","No"])
    .setRequired(false);

  const cockpitId = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("COCKPIT_ID");
  if (cockpitId) {
    form.setDestination(FormApp.DestinationType.SPREADSHEET, cockpitId);
  }

  deleteTriggersByHandler_("onIntakeFormSubmit");
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("onIntakeFormSubmit").forForm(form).onFormSubmit().create();

  Logger.log("Intake form URL: " + form.getEditUrl());
  Logger.log("Public form URL: " + form.getPublishedUrl());

  return form;
}

function addFileUploadOrFallback_(form, title, required, helpText) {
  try {
    return form.addFileUploadItem().setTitle(title).setRequired(required).setHelpText(helpText);
  } catch (e) {
    return form.addParagraphTextItem()
      .setTitle(title + " — Drive link")
      .setRequired(required)
      .setHelpText("File upload could not be added by script. Paste a Google Drive link here. " + helpText);
  }
}

function onIntakeFormSubmit(e) {
  const cockpitId = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("COCKPIT_ID");
  if (!cockpitId) return;

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(cockpitId);
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName("Client Intake");
  if (!sh) return;

  const answers = {};
  e.response.getItemResponses().forEach(ir => {
    answers[ir.getItem().getTitle()] = ir.getResponse();
  });

  const now = new Date();
  const clientId = "C" + Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyyMMddHHmmss");

  const row = [
    clientId,
    now,
    answers["Name"] || "",
    answers["Organization"] || "",
    answers["Name"] || "",
    answers["Email"] || "",
    answers["WhatsApp/phone"] || "",
    answers["Tender title"] || "",
    answers["Donor/client"] || "",
    answers["Country/region"] || "",
    answers["Sector"] || "",
    answers["Submission deadline"] || "",
    answers["Submission type"] || "",
    answers["Are you bidding as lead, partner, sub-consultant, consortium member, or individual expert?"] || "",
    answers["What is your main concern about this opportunity?"] || "",
    answers["Do you have CVs ready?"] || "",
    answers["Do you have past project references?"] || "",
    answers["Do you have donor/client forms or templates?"] || "",
    answers["Do you already have a draft proposal?"] || "",
    answers["Which diagnostic do you need?"] || "",
    answers["Is budget approved for this diagnostic?"] || "",
    "Pending",
    answers["Do you have the ToR/RFP/EOI document?"] === "Yes" ? "Partial" : "No",
    answers["Do you require a confidentiality note/NDA?"] || "",
    "Submitted via Google Form"
  ];

  sh.appendRow(row);

  const email = answers["Email"];
  const name = answers["Name"] || "";
  if (email) {
    GmailApp.sendEmail(
      email,
      "TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic — Intake Received",
      "Dear " + name + ",\n\n" +
      "Thank you for submitting the TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic intake form.\n\n" +
      "I will review the information and confirm the appropriate diagnostic package, price, timeline, and required next steps.\n\n" +
      "Please ensure that the following documents are shared:\n\n" +
      "- ToR / RFP / EOI document\n" +
      "- Submission deadline\n" +
      "- Available CVs\n" +
      "- Past project references\n" +
      "- Donor/client forms or templates\n" +
      "- Existing draft, if any\n" +
      "- Intended bidding role\n\n" +
      "Work begins only after scope confirmation, payment, and receipt of required documents.\n\n" +
      "Best,\n" +
      "Syful\n" +
      "Founder, TenderProof OS / ProposalGuard\n" +
      "[Email] | [Phone] | [LinkedIn]"
    );
  }
}

function deleteTriggersByHandler_(handlerName) {
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(t => {
    if (t.getHandlerFunction() === handlerName) {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t);
    }
  });
}

/**
 * 3. GOOGLE DRIVE FOLDER BUILDER
 */
function buildDriveFolders() {
  const root = getOrCreateFolder_(DriveApp, TP.rootFolderName);
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("ROOT_FOLDER_ID", root.getId());

  [
    "01_Leads_CRM",
    "02_Offer_PDF",
    "03_Client_Intake",
    "04_Active_Diagnostics",
    "05_Templates",
    "06_Delivery_Reports",
    "07_IP_Capture",
    "08_Invoices_Terms"
  ].forEach(name => getOrCreateFolder_(root, name));

  const active = getOrCreateFolder_(root, "04_Active_Diagnostics");
  const clientFolder = getOrCreateFolder_(active, TP.sampleClientFolder);

  [
    "01_Client_Inputs",
    "02_ToR_Extraction",
    "03_Compliance_Matrix",
    "04_CV_Matcher",
    "05_Bid_NoBid_Scorecard",
    "06_Risk_Register",
    "07_Diagnostic_Report",
    "08_Client_Delivery",
    "09_IP_Capture_Anonymized"
  ].forEach(name => getOrCreateFolder_(clientFolder, name));

  Logger.log("Drive root folder URL: " + root.getUrl());
  return root;
}

function getOrCreateFolder_(parentOrDriveApp, name) {
  const folders = parentOrDriveApp.getFoldersByName(name);
  if (folders.hasNext()) return folders.next();
  return parentOrDriveApp.createFolder(name);
}

/**
 * 4. GOOGLE DOCS TEMPLATE BUILDER
 */
function buildDocsTemplates() {
  const root = buildDriveFolders();
  const templatesFolder = getOrCreateFolder_(root, "05_Templates");
  const invoiceFolder = getOrCreateFolder_(root, "08_Invoices_Terms");

  createDocFromText_(
    "TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic — One Page Offer",
    getOnePageOfferText_(),
    templatesFolder
  );

  createDocFromText_(
    "TenderProof Sales Call Sheet",
    getSalesCallSheetText_(),
    templatesFolder
  );

  createDocFromText_(
    "TenderProof Client Quote Template",
    getQuoteTemplateText_(),
    invoiceFolder
  );

  createDocFromText_(
    "TenderProof Engagement Terms",
    getEngagementTermsText_(),
    invoiceFolder
  );

  createDocFromText_(
    "TenderProof First Diagnostic Report Template",
    getDiagnosticReportTemplateText_(),
    templatesFolder
  );

  createDocFromText_(
    "TenderProof Post Delivery Upsell Email Template",
    getPostDeliveryUpsellText_(),
    templatesFolder
  );

  Logger.log("Docs templates created in: " + templatesFolder.getUrl());
}

function createDocFromText_(title, text, folder) {
  const doc = DocumentApp.create(title);
  const body = doc.getBody();
  body.clear();
  body.setText(text);
  doc.saveAndClose();

  const file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  file.moveTo(folder);
  return doc;
}

function getOnePageOfferText_() {
  return `TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic
48–72 Hour ToR, Compliance, CV Match & Bid Strategy Review for Donor-Funded Proposals

Donor-funded tenders are often lost before the full proposal is written. The ToR is complex, compliance requirements are scattered, CVs may not fully match the expert criteria, and teams often commit time before making a disciplined bid decision.

TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic gives your team a structured, fast, and commercially practical review before full proposal preparation begins.

What you receive

Within 48–72 hours, you receive a diagnostic package covering:
- ToR opportunity summary
- Bid/no-bid recommendation
- Compliance matrix
- Expert and CV matching review
- CV gap analysis
- Proposal skeleton
- Methodology outline
- Key risk flags
- Clarification question suggestions
- 45–60 minute bid strategy call

Delivery options

48-Hour Rapid Diagnostic: urgent bid/no-bid and risk snapshot.
72-Hour Deep Diagnostic: serious bidders preparing for technical proposal support.

Pricing

Bangladesh/local clients: BDT 50,000–150,000+
International clients: USD 750–2,500
Urgent delivery: rush premium may apply.

Final price depends on ToR complexity, number of CVs, deadline, and required depth.

Required documents

Please send:
- ToR / RFP / EOI / REOI document
- Submission deadline
- Available CVs
- Past project references
- Donor/client forms or templates
- Evaluation criteria, if separate
- Consortium or partner information
- Intended bidding role
- Existing draft, if available

Boundary

This diagnostic does not include full technical proposal writing, financial proposal preparation, donor portal submission, or guaranteed tender success. Full proposal support can be quoted separately after the diagnostic.

Why Syful

Mohammad Syful Hoque combines donor-funded consulting, economic analysis, CBA, policy research, education, climate, governance, private-sector development, evaluation, and Bangladesh / Global South experience. TenderProof OS helps bidders turn complex ToRs into structured, compliant, and competitive proposal systems.

Call to action

Send the ToR and deadline to request a diagnostic quote.

Mohammad Syful Hoque
Founder, TenderProof OS / ProposalGuard
Email: [Email]
WhatsApp: [Phone]
LinkedIn: [LinkedIn]`;
}

function getSalesCallSheetText_() {
  return `TenderProof Sales Call Sheet

Opening script

Thanks for taking the time, [Name]. I will keep this practical. I want to understand the tender, deadline, available documents, and whether a 48–72 hour TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic would genuinely help. If it is not a fit, I will say so directly.

12 questions

1. Which donor/client issued the ToR?
2. What is the submission deadline?
3. What stage is this: EOI, RFP, technical, financial, or expert role?
4. Are you bidding as lead, partner, sub-consultant, consortium member, or individual expert?
5. What is the main concern right now?
6. Do you already meet the eligibility requirements?
7. Do you have CVs ready?
8. Do you have relevant past project references?
9. Has a compliance matrix been prepared?
10. Has the methodology section been drafted?
11. Who will decide whether to proceed?
12. Is budget already approved for bid support?

Urgency checklist

[ ] Deadline within 2 weeks
[ ] No compliance matrix
[ ] CVs not aligned
[ ] Methodology not drafted
[ ] Expert gap exists
[ ] Shortlisted already
[ ] International partner needs local input
[ ] Proposal team overloaded

Buyer authority: ___ / 5
Ability to pay: ___ / 5
ToR/document readiness: ___ / 5
Sector fit: ___ / 5
Repeat potential: ___ / 5
Total: ___ / 30

Close probability rule

24–30: High — ask for documents and quote.
18–23: Medium — send offer note and follow up.
12–17: Low — nurture.
Below 12: Park.

Recommended offer rule

Deadline very close: 48-hour diagnostic.
Serious bid with enough documents: 72-hour diagnostic.
They ask for whole proposal: diagnostic first, then proposal sprint.
Individual expert role: CV-ToR diagnostic.
No active tender: keep on list and ask for referral.

function getEngagementTermsText_() {
  return `TenderProof Engagement Terms

1. Work starts after payment confirmation and receipt of required documents.
2. Diagnostic fees are paid 100% upfront for first-time clients.
3. Delivery timeline begins only after all required documents are received.
4. The diagnostic includes only the deliverables agreed under the selected package.
5. Full technical proposal writing is not included unless separately contracted.
6. Client is responsible for final submission to the donor/client.
7. TenderProof OS does not guarantee tender success.
8. Additional work beyond agreed scope will be quoted separately.
9. Urgent delivery may include a rush premium.
10. Client is responsible for accuracy of submitted CVs, references, credentials, and organizational information.

Scope boundary

The diagnostic includes:
- ToR review
- bid/no-bid assessment
- compliance matrix
- CV/expert match review
- methodology outline
- proposal skeleton
- risk flags
- one diagnostic call

The diagnostic does not include:
- full technical proposal drafting
- financial proposal preparation
- full CV rewriting
- graphic design/formatting
- donor portal submission
- partner negotiation
- guaranteed tender success
- fabricated credentials or experience

No-refund clause

Because the diagnostic is time-sensitive knowledge work, no refund applies after work has started. Work is considered started once the ToR, CVs, or supporting documents have been reviewed.

Refund may be considered only if the client cancels before document review begins.

Confidentiality note

All client documents, ToRs, CVs, proposal drafts, project references, and internal discussions will be treated as confidential. TenderProof OS will not share client documents externally without written permission.

Anonymized learning may be captured internally to improve templates, compliance checklists, and proposal quality systems, without disclosing client identity or confidential project information.

Ethical boundary

TenderProof OS does not fabricate credentials, project experience, CV claims, eligibility information, donor references, contract values, or institutional experience. All proposal content must be based on accurate and verifiable information provided by the client.`;
}

# 6. SCRIPT LIMITATIONS

| Limitation | What to do |
|---|---|
| Google Forms file upload may fail on some accounts | Script creates fallback Drive-link fields if upload fails |
| Google Forms conditional logic is limited by script | Set final branching manually in the Form editor |
| Running `buildCockpit()` again creates a new spreadsheet | Use the latest created Cockpit URL from logs |
| Running `buildDocsTemplates()` again creates duplicate Docs | Delete older template copies if needed |
| Gmail auto-reply requires permission | Approve Gmail permission during first run |
| Form responses also create a default response sheet | Keep it; the trigger also copies core fields to `Client Intake` |
| Dashboard manual fields remain manual | Fill delivery, upsell, retainer, template, and learning fields weekly |
````

````
# 1. CODE AUDIT

| Issue | Severity | Where It Happens | Why It Matters | Fix |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| Re-running `buildCockpit()` creates duplicate spreadsheets | High | Previous cockpit builder | Multiple files named the same can confuse execution | Patched code reuses existing spreadsheet from script properties or Drive search |
| Re-running docs builder creates duplicate Google Docs | Medium | Previous `createDocFromText_()` | Creates clutter and outdated templates | Patched code updates existing docs in the template folders |
| Form file upload can fail or restrict public access | High | `form.addFileUploadItem()` | File upload questions require Google sign-in and can fail on some account types | Patched code defaults to Drive-link fields; optional file upload can be enabled |
| Google Form conditional branching is limited | Medium | Form builder | Apps Script can create branching, but complex show/hide logic is fragile | Patched code uses safe non-blocking document link fields and gives manual branching notes |
| Gmail auto-reply can fail and stop trigger | Medium | Previous `GmailApp.sendEmail()` | Missing permission or daily quota could break form handling | Patched code uses `MailApp.sendEmail()` inside `try/catch`; failure does not stop form-to-sheet copy |
| Duplicate form submission append risk | High | `onIntakeFormSubmit()` | Same trigger can append duplicate rows if retried | Patched code stores `Form Response ID` and skips if already copied |
| Trigger duplicates | Medium | Previous trigger creation | Multiple triggers can cause duplicate rows/emails | Patched code deletes old trigger before installing a new one |
| Dashboard formulas used fragile column references | High | Weekly Dashboard formulas | Wrong counts if headers shift | Patched code uses final fixed column map and comments |
| Client Intake lacked source fields | Medium | Client Intake tab | Hard to identify form-submitted vs manually entered leads | Patched code adds `Source`, `Form Response ID`, `Form Submitted At` |
| Form response destination creates default response sheet | Low | `form.setDestination()` | Google automatically creates a Form Responses tab | Confirmed; script also copies key fields to `Client Intake` |
| Dates may display inconsistently | Low | Sheet sample rows and formulas | Date columns may show full timestamp | Patched code applies date formatting to date-like columns |
| Dropdown validation over too few rows | Low | Validation setup | Future entries beyond range lack dropdowns | Patched code applies validations to 1,000 rows |
| `createFilter()` can fail if filter already exists | Low | Sheet formatting | Runtime warning/error on rerun | Patched code catches filter creation errors safely |
| Existing sheet tabs not deleted | Low | Cockpit reuse | Old extra tabs may remain | Intentional: avoids deleting Form Responses sheet |
| Authorization blocked by sensitive scopes | Medium | Drive/Docs/Forms/Mail scopes | First run needs approvals | Runbook includes permission steps and fallback |

---

# 2. FINAL PATCHED `Code.gs`

```javascript
/*******************************************************
 * TenderProof OS Google Workspace Build Kit
 *
 * Recommended first run:
 *   1) runFullSetup()
 *
 * Safer run modes:
 *   - runFullSetup()
 *   - runCockpitOnly()
 *   - runDocsAndFoldersOnly()
 *
 * What reruns do:
 *   - Cockpit: reuses existing spreadsheet if this script has created/found one.
 *   - Folders: get-or-create; avoids duplicate folders where possible.
 *   - Docs: get-or-create by title inside the target folder; updates content.
 *   - Form: reuses existing form from script properties, clears items, rebuilds.
 *******************************************************/

const TP_CONFIG = {
  cockpitName: "TenderProof OS Execution Cockpit",
  rootFolderName: "TenderProof_OS",
  sampleClientFolder: "ClientName_TenderShortName",
  maxRows: 1000,

  // Safer default: false.
  // File uploads can restrict forms and require Google sign-in.
  // Set true only if you specifically want Google Form file upload items.
  useFileUploads: false
};

/**
 * RUN FIRST for full setup.
 */
function runFullSetup() {
  const ss = buildCockpit_();
  const root = buildDriveFolders_();
  const docs = buildDocsTemplates_();
  const form = buildIntakeForm_();

  logCreatedUrls_(ss, root, docs, form);

  Logger.log("FULL SETUP COMPLETE.");
  Logger.log("Cockpit URL: " + ss.getUrl());
  Logger.log("Drive Root URL: " + root.getUrl());
  Logger.log("Form Edit URL: " + form.getEditUrl());
  Logger.log("Form Public URL: " + form.getPublishedUrl());
}

/**
 * Use this if you only want the Google Sheet cockpit.
 */
function runCockpitOnly() {
  const ss = buildCockpit_();
  Logger.log("COCKPIT ONLY COMPLETE.");
  Logger.log("Cockpit URL: " + ss.getUrl());
}

/**
 * Use this if the Sheet/Form already exists and you only want folders + docs.
 */
function runDocsAndFoldersOnly() {
  const root = buildDriveFolders_();
  const docs = buildDocsTemplates_();
  Logger.log("DOCS AND FOLDERS COMPLETE.");
  Logger.log("Drive Root URL: " + root.getUrl());
  docs.forEach(d => Logger.log(d.name + ": " + d.url));
}

/**
 * Optional helper if you need to rebuild the form only.
 */
function runFormOnly() {
  const form = buildIntakeForm_();
  Logger.log("FORM ONLY COMPLETE.");
  Logger.log("Form Edit URL: " + form.getEditUrl());
  Logger.log("Form Public URL: " + form.getPublishedUrl());
}

/**
 * RESET WARNING:
 * This only clears stored IDs from this Apps Script project.
 * It does NOT delete your existing Google Sheet, Form, Docs, or folders.
 * After running this, the next setup may create or find files again.
 */
function resetTenderProofScriptProperties_WARNING_ONLY() {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteAllProperties();
  Logger.log("Script properties cleared. Existing Drive files were NOT deleted.");
}

/* =====================================================
   1. GOOGLE SHEETS — COCKPIT BUILDER
===================================================== */

function buildCockpit_() {
  const ss = getOrCreateCockpitSpreadsheet_();
  const definitions = getSheetDefinitions_();

  definitions.forEach(def => {
    const sh = getOrCreateSheet_(ss, def.name);
    sh.clear();

    ensureRowsAndColumns_(sh, TP_CONFIG.maxRows, def.headers.length);

    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, def.headers.length).setValues([def.headers]);

    if (def.sampleRows && def.sampleRows.length > 0) {
      sh.getRange(2, 1, def.sampleRows.length, def.headers.length).setValues(def.sampleRows);
    }

    formatSheet_(sh, def.headers.length);
    applyValidations_(sh, def.headers, def.validations || {});
    applyDateFormats_(sh, def.headers);
  });

  applyCockpitFormulas_(ss);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("COCKPIT_ID", ss.getId());
  return ss;
}

function getOrCreateCockpitSpreadsheet_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const existingId = props.getProperty("COCKPIT_ID");

  if (existingId) {
    try {
      return SpreadsheetApp.openById(existingId);
    } catch (err) {
      Logger.log("Stored cockpit ID could not be opened. Will search by name. Error: " + err);
    }
  }

  const files = DriveApp.getFilesByName(TP_CONFIG.cockpitName);
  while (files.hasNext()) {
    const file = files.next();
    if (file.getMimeType() === MimeType.GOOGLE_SHEETS) {
      props.setProperty("COCKPIT_ID", file.getId());
      return SpreadsheetApp.openById(file.getId());
    }
  }

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.create(TP_CONFIG.cockpitName);
  props.setProperty("COCKPIT_ID", ss.getId());
  return ss;
}

function getOrCreateSheet_(ss, sheetName) {
  let sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (sh) return sh;

  const sheets = ss.getSheets();

  // Reuse the default blank first sheet if possible.
  if (sheets.length === 1 && sheets[0].getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheets[0].setName(sheetName);
    return sheets[0];
  }

  return ss.insertSheet(sheetName);
}

function ensureRowsAndColumns_(sh, minRows, minCols) {
  if (sh.getMaxRows() < minRows) {
    sh.insertRowsAfter(sh.getMaxRows(), minRows - sh.getMaxRows());
  }
  if (sh.getMaxColumns() < minCols) {
    sh.insertColumnsAfter(sh.getMaxColumns(), minCols - sh.getMaxColumns());
  }
}

function getSheetDefinitions_() {
  const today = new Date();
  const d2 = addDays_(today, 2);
  const d3 = addDays_(today, 3);
  const d11 = addDays_(today, 11);
  const d14 = addDays_(today, 14);
  const d23 = addDays_(today, 23);

  const sheets = [];

  let headers = [
````
