````
function applyCockpitFormulas_(ss) {
  const leads = ss.getSheetByName("Leads CRM");
  if (leads) {
    leads.getRange("U2:U" + TP_CONFIG.maxRows)
      .setFormulaR1C1('=IF(COUNTA(RC[-4]:RC[-2])=0,"",SUM(RC[-4]:RC[-2]))');
  }

  const tenders = ss.getSheetByName("Tender Tracker");
  if (tenders) {
    tenders.getRange("I2:I" + TP_CONFIG.maxRows)
      .setFormulaR1C1('=IF(RC[-1]="","",RC[-1]-TODAY())');
  }

  const score = ss.getSheetByName("Bid/No-Bid Scorecard");
  if (score) {
    score.getRange("E2:E" + TP_CONFIG.maxRows)
      .setFormulaR1C1('=IF(OR(RC[-2]="",RC[-1]=""),"",RC[-2]*RC[-1])');
  }

  const dash = ss.getSheetByName("Weekly Dashboard");
  if (!dash) return;

  for (let r = 2; r <= 53; r++) {
    dash.getRange(r, 3).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!B:B,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!B:B,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 4).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!W:W,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!W:W,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 5).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!Z:Z,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!Z:Z,"<="&B${r})+COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AA:AA,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AA:AA,"<="&B${r})+COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AB:AB,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AB:AB,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 6).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AC:AC,"Yes",'Leads CRM'!AD:AD,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AD:AD,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 7).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AE:AE,"Yes",'Leads CRM'!AF:AF,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AF:AF,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 8).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AG:AG,"Yes",'Leads CRM'!AH:AH,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AH:AH,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 9).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Leads CRM'!AI:AI,"Yes",'Leads CRM'!AJ:AJ,">="&A${r},'Leads CRM'!AJ:AJ,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 10).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('Pricing Tracker'!G:G,"*Diagnostic*",'Pricing Tracker'!O:O,"Yes",'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,">="&A${r},'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 11).setFormula(`=SUMIFS('Pricing Tracker'!K:K,'Pricing Tracker'!I:I,"BDT",'Pricing Tracker'!O:O,"Yes",'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,">="&A${r},'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 12).setFormula(`=SUMIFS('Pricing Tracker'!K:K,'Pricing Tracker'!I:I,"USD",'Pricing Tracker'!O:O,"Yes",'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,">="&A${r},'Pricing Tracker'!P:P,"<="&B${r})`);
    dash.getRange(r, 18).setFormula(`=COUNTIFS('IP Capture Database'!B:B,">="&A${r},'IP Capture Database'!B:B,"<="&B${r})`);
  }
}

function formatSheet_(sh, lastCol) {
  sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  const header = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol);
  header.setFontWeight("bold")
    .setBackground("#1f4e78")
    .setFontColor("#ffffff")
    .setWrap(true);

  sh.getRange(1, 1, Math.max(1, sh.getLastRow()), lastCol).setWrap(true);

  try {
    if (!sh.getFilter()) {
      sh.getRange(1, 1, Math.max(2, sh.getLastRow()), lastCol).createFilter();
    }
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log("Filter skipped for " + sh.getName() + ": " + err);
  }

  sh.setRowHeight(1, 42);

  try {
    sh.autoResizeColumns(1, Math.min(lastCol, 12));
  } catch (err) {}
}

function applyValidations_(sh, headers, validations) {
  Object.keys(validations).forEach(header => {
    const col = headers.indexOf(header) + 1;
    if (col <= 0) return;

    const rule = SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
      .requireValueInList(validations[header], true)
      .setAllowInvalid(false)
      .build();

    sh.getRange(2, col, TP_CONFIG.maxRows - 1, 1).setDataValidation(rule);
  });
}

function applyDateFormats_(sh, headers) {
  const dateNames = [
    "Date Added","Deadline","Date","Submission Deadline","Message Sent Date","Last Contact Date",
    "Next Follow-Up Date","Follow-Up 1 Sent Date","Follow-Up 2 Sent Date","Final Follow-Up Sent Date",
    "Reply Date","Call Date","Call Completed Date","Quote Date","Payment Date","Invoice Date",
    "Follow-Up Date","Date Captured","Week Start Date","Week End Date","Form Submitted At"
  ];

  headers.forEach((h, idx) => {
    if (dateNames.indexOf(h) !== -1) {
      sh.getRange(2, idx + 1, TP_CONFIG.maxRows - 1, 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy-mm-dd");
    }
  });
}

function row_(headers, obj) {
  return headers.map(h => obj[h] !== undefined ? obj[h] : "");
}

function addDays_(date, days) {
  const d = new Date(date);
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + days);
  return d;
}

/* =====================================================
   2. GOOGLE FORM BUILDER
===================================================== */

function buildIntakeForm_() {
  const form = getOrCreateForm_();

  clearFormItems_(form);

  form.setTitle("TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic — Client Intake Form");
  form.setDescription(
    "Please complete this form to request a 48–72 hour diagnostic for a donor-funded tender or proposal opportunity. " +
    "The diagnostic may include ToR analysis, bid/no-bid recommendation, compliance matrix, CV/expert matching, methodology outline, and proposal risk flags. " +
    "Full proposal writing is separate and quoted after the diagnostic or after scope review.\n\n" +
    "Work begins only after scope confirmation, payment, and receipt of required documents."
  );

  form.setConfirmationMessage(
    "Thank you. Your TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic request has been received. " +
    "Please also send the ToR and deadline directly to [Email] or WhatsApp [Phone] if not uploaded here. " +
    "I will review the scope and confirm the package, price, timeline, and payment terms before work begins."
  );

  form.addSectionHeaderItem().setTitle("Section 1: Client information");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Name").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Your full name.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Organization").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Firm, NGO, think tank, or individual consultant name.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Role/designation").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Example: Managing Director, Proposal Manager, Team Leader.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Email").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Use the email for follow-up and documents.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("WhatsApp/phone").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Include country code if outside Bangladesh.");

  form.addPageBreakItem().setTitle("Section 2: Tender information");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Tender title").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Copy the title from the ToR/RFP.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Donor/client").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Example: ADB, World Bank, EU, GIZ, USAID, UN.");
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Country/region").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Country or region of assignment.");
  form.addListItem()
    .setTitle("Sector")
    .setChoiceValues(["Education","Governance","Climate","Infrastructure","Private Sector Development","Evaluation/M&E","Public Finance","CBA/Economics","Other"])
    .setRequired(true);
  form.addDateItem().setTitle("Submission deadline").setRequired(true).setHelpText("Final submission deadline.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Submission type")
    .setChoiceValues(["EOI","REOI","RFP","Technical Proposal","Financial Proposal","Individual Consultant","Other"])
    .setRequired(true);

  form.addPageBreakItem().setTitle("Section 3: Bidding role and concern");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Are you bidding as lead, partner, sub-consultant, consortium member, or individual expert?")
    .setChoiceValues(["Lead","Partner","Sub-consultant","Consortium Member","Individual Expert","Not Sure"])
    .setRequired(true);
  form.addParagraphTextItem()
    .setTitle("What is your main concern about this opportunity?")
    .setRequired(true)
    .setHelpText("Example: compliance, CV fit, methodology, deadline, expert gaps, bid/no-bid decision.");
  form.addTextItem()
    .setTitle("Who will decide whether to proceed with this diagnostic?")
    .setRequired(true)
    .setHelpText("Name or role of decision-maker.");

  form.addPageBreakItem().setTitle("Section 4: Available documents");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Do you have the ToR/RFP/EOI document?")
    .setChoiceValues(["Yes","No"])
    .setRequired(true)
    .setHelpText("If no, please provide expected availability in the final note.");

  addUploadOrLinkQuestion_(form, "Upload or link ToR/RFP/EOI document", false, "Upload or paste a Google Drive link. Required before paid work begins.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you have CVs ready?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No","Partially"]).setRequired(true);
  addUploadOrLinkQuestion_(form, "Upload or link available CVs", false, "Upload or paste a Google Drive link if available.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you have past project references?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No","Partially"]).setRequired(true);
  addUploadOrLinkQuestion_(form, "Upload or link past project references", false, "Upload or paste a Google Drive link if available.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you have donor/client forms or templates?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No","Not Sure"]).setRequired(true);
  addUploadOrLinkQuestion_(form, "Upload or link donor/client forms/templates", false, "Upload or paste a Google Drive link if available.");

  form.addPageBreakItem().setTitle("Section 5: Existing work and package");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you already have a draft proposal?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No"]).setRequired(true);
  addUploadOrLinkQuestion_(form, "Upload or link existing draft", false, "Upload or paste a Google Drive link if available.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Which diagnostic do you need?")
    .setChoiceValues(["48-hour Rapid Diagnostic","72-hour Deep Diagnostic","Not Sure"])
    .setRequired(true);
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem()
    .setTitle("Is budget approved for this diagnostic?")
    .setChoiceValues(["Yes","No","Need Approval"])
    .setRequired(true);
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("Preferred payment method").setRequired(false).setHelpText("Bank transfer, mobile banking, international transfer, etc.");
  form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle("Do you require a confidentiality note/NDA?").setChoiceValues(["Yes","No"]).setRequired(true);
  form.addTextItem().setTitle("When do you expect the ToR/RFP to be available, if not available now?").setRequired(false);
  form.addParagraphTextItem().setTitle("Any other instruction or concern?").setRequired(false);

  const cockpitId = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("COCKPIT_ID");
  if (cockpitId) {
    try {
      form.setDestination(FormApp.DestinationType.SPREADSHEET, cockpitId);
    } catch (err) {
      Logger.log("Could not set Form destination. Error: " + err);
    }
  }

  deleteTriggersByHandler_("onIntakeFormSubmit");
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("onIntakeFormSubmit").forForm(form).onFormSubmit().create();

  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("INTAKE_FORM_ID", form.getId());

  return form;
}

function getOrCreateForm_() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const existingId = props.getProperty("INTAKE_FORM_ID");

  if (existingId) {
    try {
      return FormApp.openById(existingId);
    } catch (err) {
      Logger.log("Stored form ID could not be opened. Will create new form. Error: " + err);
    }
  }

  const form = FormApp.create("TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic — Client Intake Form");
  props.setProperty("INTAKE_FORM_ID", form.getId());
  return form;
}

function clearFormItems_(form) {
  const items = form.getItems();
  for (let i = items.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    form.deleteItem(items[i]);
  }
}

function addUploadOrLinkQuestion_(form, title, required, helpText) {
  if (TP_CONFIG.useFileUploads) {
    try {
      return form.addFileUploadItem()
        .setTitle(title)
        .setRequired(required)
        .setHelpText(helpText);
    } catch (err) {
      Logger.log("File upload unavailable for '" + title + "'. Falling back to paragraph link field. Error: " + err);
    }
  }

  return form.addParagraphTextItem()
    .setTitle(title + " — Google Drive link or note")
    .setRequired(required)
    .setHelpText(helpText + " If no file is ready, write 'Not available yet'.");
}

function onIntakeFormSubmit(e) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  try {
    lock.waitLock(30000);

    const cockpitId = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("COCKPIT_ID");
    if (!cockpitId || !e || !e.response) return;

    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(cockpitId);
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName("Client Intake");
    if (!sh) return;

    const response = e.response;
    const responseId = response.getId ? response.getId() : Utilities.getUuid();
    const submittedAt = response.getTimestamp ? response.getTimestamp() : new Date();

    const headers = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const responseIdCol = headers.indexOf("Form Response ID") + 1;

    if (responseIdCol > 0 && sh.getLastRow() > 1) {
      const existingIds = sh.getRange(2, responseIdCol, sh.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
      if (existingIds.indexOf(responseId) !== -1) {
        Logger.log("Duplicate form response skipped: " + responseId);
        return;
      }
    }

    const answers = {};
    response.getItemResponses().forEach(ir => {
      answers[ir.getItem().getTitle()] = ir.getResponse();
    });

    const clientId = "C" + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyyMMddHHmmss");
    const rowObj = {
      "Client ID": clientId,
      "Date": submittedAt,
      "Client Name": answers["Name"] || "",
      "Organization": answers["Organization"] || "",
      "Contact Person": answers["Name"] || "",
      "Email": answers["Email"] || "",
      "WhatsApp": answers["WhatsApp/phone"] || "",
      "Tender Title": answers["Tender title"] || "",
      "Donor/Client": answers["Donor/client"] || "",
      "Country": answers["Country/region"] || "",
      "Sector": answers["Sector"] || "",
      "Submission Deadline": answers["Submission deadline"] || "",
      "Submission Type": answers["Submission type"] || "",
      "Bidding Role": answers["Are you bidding as lead, partner, sub-consultant, consortium member, or individual expert?"] || "",
      "Main Concern": answers["What is your main concern about this opportunity?"] || "",
      "CVs Available": answers["Do you have CVs ready?"] || "",
      "Past References Available": answers["Do you have past project references?"] || "",
      "Forms/Templates Available": answers["Do you have donor/client forms or templates?"] || "",
      "Existing Draft": answers["Do you already have a draft proposal?"] || "",
      "Preferred Diagnostic": normalizeDiagnostic_(answers["Which diagnostic do you need?"] || ""),
      "Budget Approved": answers["Is budget approved for this diagnostic?"] || "",
      "Payment Status": "Pending",
      "Documents Received": answers["Do you have the ToR/RFP/EOI document?"] === "Yes" ? "Partial" : "No",
      "Confidentiality Required": answers["Do you require a confidentiality note/NDA?"] || "",
      "Notes": "Submitted via Google Form",
      "Source": "Google Form",
      "Form Response ID": responseId,
      "Form Submitted At": submittedAt
    };

    const row = headers.map(h => rowObj[h] !== undefined ? rowObj[h] : "");
    sh.appendRow(row);

    try {
      const email = answers["Email"];
      const name = answers["Name"] || "";
      if (email) {
        MailApp.sendEmail({
          to: email,
          subject: "TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic — Intake Received",
          body:
            "Dear " + name + ",\n\n" +
            "Thank you for submitting the TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic intake form.\n\n" +
            "I will review the information and confirm the appropriate diagnostic package, price, timeline, and required next steps.\n\n" +
            "Please ensure that the following documents are shared:\n\n" +
            "- ToR / RFP / EOI document\n" +
            "- Submission deadline\n" +
            "- Available CVs\n" +
            "- Past project references\n" +
            "- Donor/client forms or templates\n" +
            "- Existing draft, if any\n" +
            "- Intended bidding role\n\n" +
            "Work begins only after scope confirmation, payment, and receipt of required documents.\n\n" +
            "Best,\n" +
            "Syful\n" +
            "Founder, TenderProof OS / ProposalGuard\n" +
            "[Email] | [Phone] | [LinkedIn]"
        });
      }
    } catch (mailErr) {
      Logger.log("Auto-reply email failed but form copy succeeded. Error: " + mailErr);
    }

  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log("onIntakeFormSubmit failed: " + err);
  } finally {
    try {
      lock.releaseLock();
    } catch (releaseErr) {}
  }
}

function normalizeDiagnostic_(value) {
  if (value.indexOf("48") !== -1) return "48h";
  if (value.indexOf("72") !== -1) return "72h";
  return value || "Not Sure";
}

function deleteTriggersByHandler_(handlerName) {
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(t => {
    if (t.getHandlerFunction() === handlerName) {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t);
    }
  });
}

/* =====================================================
   3. DRIVE FOLDER BUILDER
===================================================== */

function buildDriveFolders_() {
  const root = getOrCreateFolder_(DriveApp, TP_CONFIG.rootFolderName);
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("ROOT_FOLDER_ID", root.getId());

  [
    "01_Leads_CRM",
    "02_Offer_PDF",
    "03_Client_Intake",
    "04_Active_Diagnostics",
    "05_Templates",
    "06_Delivery_Reports",
    "07_IP_Capture",
    "08_Invoices_Terms"
  ].forEach(name => getOrCreateFolder_(root, name));

  const active = getOrCreateFolder_(root, "04_Active_Diagnostics");
  const clientFolder = getOrCreateFolder_(active, TP_CONFIG.sampleClientFolder);

  [
    "01_Client_Inputs",
    "02_ToR_Extraction",
    "03_Compliance_Matrix",
    "04_CV_Matcher",
    "05_Bid_NoBid_Scorecard",
    "06_Risk_Register",
    "07_Diagnostic_Report",
    "08_Client_Delivery",
    "09_IP_Capture_Anonymized"
  ].forEach(name => getOrCreateFolder_(clientFolder, name));

  return root;
}

function getOrCreateFolder_(parentOrDriveApp, name) {
  const folders = parentOrDriveApp.getFoldersByName(name);
  if (folders.hasNext()) return folders.next();
  return parentOrDriveApp.createFolder(name);
}

/* =====================================================
   4. DOCS TEMPLATE BUILDER
===================================================== */

function buildDocsTemplates_() {
  const root = buildDriveFolders_();
  const templatesFolder = getOrCreateFolder_(root, "05_Templates");
  const invoiceFolder = getOrCreateFolder_(root, "08_Invoices_Terms");

  const docs = [];

  docs.push(createOrUpdateDocInFolder_(
    templatesFolder,
    "TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic — One Page Offer",
    getOnePageOfferText_()
  ));

  docs.push(createOrUpdateDocInFolder_(
    templatesFolder,
    "TenderProof Sales Call Sheet",
    getSalesCallSheetText_()
  ));

  docs.push(createOrUpdateDocInFolder_(
    invoiceFolder,
    "TenderProof Client Quote Template",
    getQuoteTemplateText_()
  ));

  docs.push(createOrUpdateDocInFolder_(
    invoiceFolder,
    "TenderProof Engagement Terms",
    getEngagementTermsText_()
  ));

  docs.push(createOrUpdateDocInFolder_(
    templatesFolder,
    "TenderProof First Diagnostic Report Template",
    getDiagnosticReportTemplateText_()
  ));

  docs.push(createOrUpdateDocInFolder_(
    templatesFolder,
    "TenderProof Post Delivery Upsell Email Template",
    getPostDeliveryUpsellText_()
  ));

  return docs;
}

function createOrUpdateDocInFolder_(folder, title, text) {
  let file = null;
  const files = folder.getFilesByName(title);

  while (files.hasNext()) {
    const candidate = files.next();
    if (candidate.getMimeType() === MimeType.GOOGLE_DOCS) {
      file = candidate;
      break;
    }
  }

  let doc;

  if (file) {
    doc = DocumentApp.openById(file.getId());
  } else {
    doc = DocumentApp.create(title);
    file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
    file.moveTo(folder);
  }

  const body = doc.getBody();
  body.clear();
  body.setText(text);
  doc.saveAndClose();

  return {
    name: title,
    url: doc.getUrl()
  };
}

function getOnePageOfferText_() {
  return `TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic
48–72 Hour ToR, Compliance, CV Match & Bid Strategy Review for Donor-Funded Proposals

Donor-funded tenders are often lost before the full proposal is written. The ToR is complex, compliance requirements are scattered, CVs may not fully match the expert criteria, and teams often commit time before making a disciplined bid decision.

TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic gives your team a structured, fast, and commercially practical review before full proposal preparation begins.

What you receive

Within 48–72 hours, you receive a diagnostic package covering:
- ToR opportunity summary
- Bid/no-bid recommendation
- Compliance matrix
- Expert and CV matching review
- CV gap analysis
- Proposal skeleton
- Methodology outline
- Key risk flags
- Clarification question suggestions
- 45–60 minute bid strategy call

Delivery options

48-Hour Rapid Diagnostic: urgent bid/no-bid and risk snapshot.
72-Hour Deep Diagnostic: serious bidders preparing for technical proposal support.

Pricing

Bangladesh/local clients: BDT 50,000–150,000+
International clients: USD 750–2,500
Urgent delivery: rush premium may apply.

Final price depends on ToR complexity, number of CVs, deadline, and required depth.

Required documents

Please send:
- ToR / RFP / EOI / REOI document
- Submission deadline
- Available CVs
- Past project references
- Donor/client forms or templates
- Evaluation criteria, if separate
- Consortium or partner information
- Intended bidding role
- Existing draft, if available

Boundary

This diagnostic does not include full technical proposal writing, financial proposal preparation, donor portal submission, or guaranteed tender success. Full proposal support can be quoted separately after the diagnostic.

Why Syful

Mohammad Syful Hoque combines donor-funded consulting, economic analysis, CBA, policy research, education, climate, governance, private-sector development, evaluation, and Bangladesh / Global South experience. TenderProof OS helps bidders turn complex ToRs into structured, compliant, and competitive proposal systems.

Call to action

Send the ToR and deadline to request a diagnostic quote.

Mohammad Syful Hoque
Founder, TenderProof OS / ProposalGuard
Email: [Email]
WhatsApp: [Phone]
LinkedIn: [LinkedIn]`;
}

[ ] No compliance matrix

Scoring table

ToR/document readiness: ___ / 5

Total: ___ / 30

Close probability rule

24–30: High — ask for documents and quote.
18–23: Medium — send offer note and follow up.
12–17: Low — nurture.
Below 12: Park.

Recommended offer rule

Based on what you shared, the right first step is the TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic.

function getQuoteTemplateText_() {
  return `TenderProof Client Quote Template

Dear [Name],

Thank you for sharing the ToR and related information.

Based on the scope, I recommend:

function getEngagementTermsText_() {
  return `TenderProof Engagement Terms

1. Work starts after payment confirmation and receipt of required documents.
2. Diagnostic fees are paid 100% upfront for first-time clients.
3. Delivery timeline begins only after all required documents are received.
4. The diagnostic includes only the deliverables agreed under the selected package.
5. Full technical proposal writing is not included unless separately contracted.
6. Client is responsible for final submission to the donor/client.
7. TenderProof OS does not guarantee tender success.
8. Additional work beyond agreed scope will be quoted separately.
9. Urgent delivery may include a rush premium.
10. Client is responsible for accuracy of submitted CVs, references, credentials, and organizational information.

Scope boundary

The diagnostic includes:
- ToR review
- bid/no-bid assessment
- compliance matrix
- CV/expert match review
- methodology outline
- proposal skeleton
- risk flags
- one diagnostic call

The diagnostic does not include:
- full technical proposal drafting
- financial proposal preparation
- full CV rewriting
- graphic design/formatting
- donor portal submission
- partner negotiation
- guaranteed tender success
- fabricated credentials or experience

No-refund clause

Because the diagnostic is time-sensitive knowledge work, no refund applies after work has started. Work is considered started once the ToR, CVs, or supporting documents have been reviewed.

Refund may be considered only if the client cancels before document review begins.

Confidentiality note

All client documents, ToRs, CVs, proposal drafts, project references, and internal discussions will be treated as confidential. TenderProof OS will not share client documents externally without written permission.

Anonymized learning may be captured internally to improve templates, compliance checklists, and proposal quality systems, without disclosing client identity or confidential project information.

Ethical boundary

TenderProof OS does not fabricate credentials, project experience, CV claims, eligibility information, donor references, contract values, or institutional experience. All proposal content must be based on accurate and verifiable information provided by the client.`;
}

function logCreatedUrls_(ss, root, docs, form) {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.setProperty("LATEST_COCKPIT_URL", ss.getUrl());
  props.setProperty("LATEST_ROOT_FOLDER_URL", root.getUrl());
  props.setProperty("LATEST_FORM_EDIT_URL", form.getEditUrl());
  props.setProperty("LATEST_FORM_PUBLIC_URL", form.getPublishedUrl());

  Logger.log("=== TENDERPROOF GENERATED URLS ===");
  Logger.log("Cockpit: " + ss.getUrl());
  Logger.log("Root Folder: " + root.getUrl());
  Logger.log("Form Edit: " + form.getEditUrl());
  Logger.log("Form Public: " + form.getPublishedUrl());
  docs.forEach(d => Logger.log("Doc - " + d.name + ": " + d.url));
}

function showLatestTenderProofUrls() {
  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  Logger.log("Cockpit: " + props.getProperty("LATEST_COCKPIT_URL"));
  Logger.log("Root Folder: " + props.getProperty("LATEST_ROOT_FOLDER_URL"));
  Logger.log("Form Edit: " + props.getProperty("LATEST_FORM_EDIT_URL"));
  Logger.log("Form Public: " + props.getProperty("LATEST_FORM_PUBLIC_URL"));
}
```

# 3. SAFER RUN MODES

## Main functions

| Function | What it does | Safe to rerun? |
|---|---|---|
| `runFullSetup()` | Creates/rebuilds Sheet, Form, Drive folders, and Docs templates | Yes, but it overwrites cockpit tab contents and form questions |
| `runCockpitOnly()` | Creates/rebuilds only the Google Sheet cockpit | Yes, but it overwrites existing cockpit tab contents |
| `runDocsAndFoldersOnly()` | Creates/rebuilds Drive folders and Docs templates | Yes; folders are reused, docs are updated |
| `runFormOnly()` | Rebuilds only the intake form | Yes, but it clears and rebuilds form questions |
| `showLatestTenderProofUrls()` | Logs latest saved URLs | Yes |
| `resetTenderProofScriptProperties_WARNING_ONLY()` | Clears stored file IDs from script properties | Dangerous if used casually; does not delete files but may cause future duplicate discovery/creation |

## Reset warning

Do **not** run:

```javascript
resetTenderProofScriptProperties_WARNING_ONLY()
```

unless you intentionally want to disconnect this Apps Script project from previously generated files.

## If you run the same function twice

| Function | Rerun behavior |
|---|---|
| `runFullSetup()` | Reuses stored Sheet/Form if available; updates folders/docs; rewrites the cockpit sample data |
| `runCockpitOnly()` | Reuses stored Sheet if available; clears/rebuilds the 11 tabs |
| `runDocsAndFoldersOnly()` | Reuses folders and updates docs by title |
| `runFormOnly()` | Reuses stored Form; deletes old form items and rebuilds them |

## How to identify latest generated files

Run:

```javascript
showLatestTenderProofUrls()
```

Then open **Executions → Logs**.

---

# 4. FORM RESPONSE SAFETY

| Item | Confirmed behavior |
|---|---|
| Default Form Responses sheet | Yes. Because the form is linked to the cockpit spreadsheet, Google Forms creates a default response sheet such as `Form Responses 1`. |
| Script copy to Client Intake | Yes. The trigger also copies key fields into the `Client Intake` tab. |
| Duplicate prevention | Yes. The script stores `Form Response ID` and skips duplicate append if the same response ID already exists. |
| Timestamp | Yes. `Form Submitted At` is added to `Client Intake`. |
| Source field | Yes. `Source = Google Form` for form submissions and `Source = Manual` for sample/manual entries. |
| Gmail/Mail failure safety | Yes. Auto-reply uses `MailApp.sendEmail()` inside `try/catch`. If email fails, Client Intake copy still succeeds. |
| Trigger duplication prevention | Yes. Old `onIntakeFormSubmit` triggers are deleted before creating a new one. |
| File upload safety | Default is Drive-link paragraph fields. This avoids Google Form file-upload restrictions. |

| IP Entries Captured | `=COUNTIFS('IP Capture Database'!B:B,">="&A2,'IP Capture Database'!B:B,"<="&B2)` |

# 6. INSTALLATION RUNBOOK

## Step 1 — Create Apps Script project

1. Go to `script.google.com`.
2. Click **New project**.
3. Rename it: `TenderProof OS Build Kit`.

## Step 2 — Paste code

1. Open `Code.gs`.
2. Delete all default code.
3. Paste the patched `Code.gs` above.
4. Click **Save**.

## Step 3 — Run first function

In the function dropdown, select:

```text
runFullSetup
```

Click **Run**.

## Step 4 — Approve permissions

Approve access for:

- Google Sheets  
- Google Forms  
- Google Drive  
- Google Docs  
- Mail sending through `MailApp`  
- Apps Script triggers  

If Google shows “unverified app”:

1. Click **Advanced**.
2. Click **Go to TenderProof OS Build Kit**.
3. Click **Allow**.

## Step 5 — Check logs

After the run:

1. Go to **Executions**.
2. Open the latest run.
3. Check logs for:

```text
FULL SETUP COMPLETE.
Cockpit URL:
Drive Root URL:
Form Edit URL:
Form Public URL:
```

Or run:

```text
showLatestTenderProofUrls
```

## Step 6 — Find generated files

Look in Google Drive for:

- `TenderProof OS Execution Cockpit`
- `TenderProof_OS`
- `TenderProof Rapid Bid Diagnostic — Client Intake Form`

Inside `TenderProof_OS`, check:

- `05_Templates`
- `08_Invoices_Terms`
- `04_Active_Diagnostics/ClientName_TenderShortName`

## Step 7 — Manual fixes after creation

1. Open One-page Offer Doc.
2. Replace:
   - `[Email]`
   - `[Phone]`
   - `[LinkedIn]`
3. Export as PDF.
4. Open Intake Form.
5. Replace `[Email]` and `[Phone]` in confirmation message.
6. Optional: enable file uploads manually only if you want respondents to upload files directly.
7. Optional: manually set form branching if you want “No ToR available” to skip document questions.
8. Delete or overwrite sample rows once real data begins.

---

# 7. TROUBLESHOOTING GUIDE

| Problem | Likely cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| Authorization blocked | Google security warning | Click **Advanced → Go to TenderProof OS Build Kit → Allow** |
| File upload item fails | Account or form restrictions | Keep default Drive-link fields; do not enable `useFileUploads` |
| Mail permission denied | MailApp permission not approved | Re-run `runFullSetup()` and approve Mail permission; if still blocked, auto-reply will fail safely |
| Trigger not firing | Trigger not installed or form not submitted through public URL | Run `runFormOnly()`; check Apps Script **Triggers** for `onIntakeFormSubmit` |
| Form responses not copying to Client Intake | Missing cockpit ID or trigger issue | Run `showLatestTenderProofUrls()`, then `runFormOnly()` again |
| Duplicate Client Intake rows | Multiple triggers from old code | Patched code deletes old triggers; manually delete extra triggers in Apps Script if needed |
| Form creates `Form Responses 1` tab | Normal Google Forms behavior | Keep it; script also copies fields to `Client Intake` |
| Form upload not available | File upload disabled or account restriction | Use Drive-link fields |
| Form respondents cannot upload files | File uploads require Google sign-in | Use Drive-link fields |
| Form auto-reply not sent | Mail quota/permission issue | Client Intake copy still works; send manual email |
| Dashboard formulas show errors | Sheet locale may use semicolon instead of comma | Replace commas with semicolons if your Google Sheets locale requires it |
| Days Remaining shows date instead of number | Column formatted as date | Format `Tender Tracker!I:I` as Number |
| Weighted Score blank | Missing Weight or Score | Enter both Weight and Score |
| Duplicated docs | Old script created duplicates | Use new script; delete old duplicate docs manually |
| Duplicated folders | Existing duplicate folder names in Drive | Script reuses first folder found; manually rename/delete duplicates |
| Sheet tabs not formatted | Script interrupted before formatting | Run `runCockpitOnly()` again |
| Cannot find generated files | They are in Drive root or logs | Run `showLatestTenderProofUrls()` and check Logs |
| Existing cockpit overwritten | Rerun behavior | Do not rerun `runCockpitOnly()` after entering real data unless you have backed it up |

6. Create engagement terms Doc.
7. Send first 10 warm messages.

# 10. FINAL SAFETY CHECK

| Check | Answer |
|---|---|
| Code safe to run? | Yes, with standard Google permissions approved |
| Which function to run first? | `runFullSetup()` |
| What not to touch before first run? | Do not edit helper functions, constants, formulas, or trigger handler |
| What can you safely edit before first run? | Only `TP_CONFIG.useFileUploads`, but leave it `false` for safer launch |
| What to replace manually after setup? | `[Email]`, `[Phone]`, `[LinkedIn]`, sample client rows as needed |
| What not to rerun after entering real data? | `runCockpitOnly()` unless you have backed up your data |
| What happens if `runFullSetup()` is rerun? | Existing cockpit is reused and rebuilt; tabs may be overwritten |
| What happens if `runDocsAndFoldersOnly()` is rerun? | Folders reused; docs updated by title |
| What happens if `runFormOnly()` is rerun? | Existing form is cleared and rebuilt; trigger is refreshed |
| Immediate action after setup | Export offer PDF, update LinkedIn, send 10 warm messages, log contacts, set follow-ups |
````
